> **⚙️ STANDING CONVENTION — D-121: EVERY QUESTION AND RECOMMENDATION LEADS WITH TWO NAMED LENSES (Joe, locked 2026-07-19).** Before any option list reaches Joe and before any recommendation is made, state **① user-visible impact PER OPTION** — what does a person using XGen see, feel, or come to believe? — then **② resource cost** (build time, bundle size, maintenance and test surface, future work created). Architectural elegance, symmetry and implementation convenience are **tertiary**: still recorded, never leading. **⚠️ ADDITIONAL LENSES, NOT A REPLACEMENT** — D-065, D6, N-091, D-110/D-111, D-067, the no-anonymity core and GDPR discipline stand **undiminished**; D-121 adds a pass, it does not mint a trump card. **⚠️ A COLLISION GOES TO JOE UNRESOLVED, NAMED AS A COLLISION** — never silently traded, above all where identity, the wire, or anything irreversible is touched: *a rule that can be outranked by an appeal to user experience is not a rule, and the trades this project exists to refuse are exactly the ones that feel good to a user in the moment.* **⚠️ "NO USER-FACING IMPACT" IS A LEGAL AND EXPECTED ANSWER** — say it plainly and decide on cost; *a manufactured UX rationale is worse than none, because it launders an internal preference as a user's interest.* **THE REASON GOVERNS THE APPLICATION:** Joe locks architecture but cannot independently verify most technical claims put to him — these two axes are the ones he CAN judge, so stating them is what makes a recommendation **checkable rather than trusted blind**. **🔑 THE EVIDENCE (J-559):** Chat recommended the D2 composer read the selection bus on a sound architectural argument; **Joe asked what it would do to the user's experience**, and reading `stream-panel.svelte` showed **R5 latches the room** — so the composer would have **greyed out while the user was still looking at the conversation**, on ordinary navigation, unexplained. **The recommendation flipped.** *The architecture argument was not wrong; it answered a question that ranks below the one that decides.* **PHASE-0 CONSEQUENCE:** the user-visible consequence must be **GROUNDED, not reasoned** — read the code, run the probe, say what the user sees; *a principle that says "decide by user impact" without a step that MEASURES it merely relocates the guessing.* **CLAUDE'S HONEST BOUNDARY:** resource cost is **measurable** and must be measured rather than estimated; **user experience is only ever INFERRED** — Claude never observes a user, so on many such calls **Joe is the better judge**, which is exactly why they are stated for him instead of acted on silently.

> **⚙️ STANDING CONVENTION — DESIGNATION COLLISIONS AND ADDENDA: SEE `DECISIONS.md` → D-134 (Joe, confirmed + amended 2026-07-29, J-619). THAT ENTRY IS THE RULE; THIS IS A SIGNPOST.** Applies to `J-` · `D-` · `N-` · `M-` alike. 🔒 **Designations are ISSUED UNIQUE** — the lettered split is a **repair applied at revision**, never an issuing path. **① COLLISION SPLIT:** one designation issued more than once for unrelated things → copies take `a`, `b`, `c`… **in the order they appear IN THE RECORD**, and the bare number is **RETIRED** (7 of 7 in `DECISIONS.md`: `D-030 · 031 · 037 · 038 · 039 · 055 · 056`). **② ADDENDUM:** a follow-on that extends or corrects an existing record takes the next letter and the bare number **SURVIVES** (`N-124` + `a` + `b`; `M-RP2.30` + `a`; `M-RP2.31` + `a`). 🔑 **DISCRIMINATOR — ask whether the bare number still exists.** Present ⇒ addenda. Absent ⇒ collision split. **No third notation is needed, which is why retiring the bare number is NORMATIVE.** 🛑 **`a` = FIRST IN THE RECORD, NOT FIRST IN THE FILE** — in a newest-first file (`JOURNAL_ARCHIVE.md`) the **higher line number is the earlier record**. 🛑 **NEVER renumber one side to a free number** — every existing citation would silently point at the wrong record; every citation of a retired bare number is re-pointed **individually**.

> **⚙️ STANDING CONVENTION — NO BACKTICKS IN ANY POWERSHELL GIVEN TO JOE (locked 2026-07-17).** Joe hates backtick (`` ` ``) line-continuation — a stray trailing space silently breaks it, and it is not a real character for this purpose. When Claude (Chat **or** Clair) prepares a commit/push or any PS block for Joe: put each command (each `git …`) on **ONE physical line** — multiple `-m` flags on the same line — **or** use a here-string (`@" … "@`, opener at line-end, closing `"@` at column 0) for long multi-paragraph commit bodies. **No backtick line-continuation, ever.** (Git workflow otherwise unchanged: explicit `git add <file>` per file, never `git add .`; `git status` before commit; Joe pushes.)

> **🎨 APPEARANCE MODEL — the CSS layer stack is CANONICAL (J-504/J-505, `ui/docs/xgen-css-layer-model.md` v1.1). READ IT BEFORE TOUCHING ANY GLYPH OR SKIN RULE.** The *appearance* sibling of `xgen-region-dock-model.md` (which owns *layout*). **The stack, Joe-locked:** `theme-*.css` (custom skin, the override layer) → **`skin.css` + `glyphs.generated.css` — ONE layer, the default skin, split by WHO WRITES IT** (hand vs generated) → the normalizes (**reset, not skin**). **A theme overrides a glyph exactly the way it overrides a colour — the cascade IS the mechanism.** **D-108: a glyph is a SKIN TOKEN — `core` owns the NAME (content), the skin owns the SHAPE (geometry).** A component never writes geometry, for the same reason it never writes a colour (N-025/N-090). **CDP-proven:** CSS **`d:`** works in WebView2 · **`var()` resolves inside a custom-property value** → **ONE** skin rule (`.icon path { d: var(--g) }`) serves every icon · a later stylesheet **redraws any glyph with zero component change**. **D-109: the Chromium/WebView2 `d:` dependency is DELIBERATE and NOT hedged** — the WebKit exit is the `-url` form the bank already emits.

> **🔒 D-110 — THE SPACE-THEME BAN. A TRUST BOUNDARY, NOT A STYLE RULE. Ch6 §6.2 REWRITTEN + §6.3.1/§6.3.2 NEW (Ch6 v0.5).** **A SPACE MAY RE-COLOUR. A SPACE MAY NOT RE-DRAW, AND MAY NOT RE-LAYOUT.** Ch6 §6.3's Layer 3 is a theme **declared by a Space OWNER and delivered over the wire** — unrestricted, it would let a Space owner **redraw a lock, a warning, a verified mark, or the AI badge (§6.13)**: icon spoofing in an identity-verified protocol. Colour (incl. the glyph **tint**) ✅ · **geometry (`--glyph-*`, `--glyph-*-url`) ❌** · layout/metrics ❌ · **everything not on the allowlist ❌ by default** (allowlist, never denylist). **🔑 TWO NORMATIVE CONSEQUENCES, both binding on future code:** **(a) A KEY ALLOWLIST ALONE IS THEATRE** — a Space theme is a key→value **map**, but a client that **string-concatenates a stylesheet** lets a malicious **VALUE** escape its declaration and inject arbitrary CSS. **MANDATORY: apply via `element.style.setProperty()` (CSSOM cannot break out) + validate with `CSS.supports()` + scope to the Space subtree — ALL THREE; any one alone is insufficient.** **NEVER interpolate a wire value into a `<style>` text node.** **(b) THE BAN BINDS THE GENERATOR BACK** — **`--glyph-*-url` MUST be emitted COLOUR-FREE** (a `currentColor` mask), because a data-URI with colour baked in **fuses colour and geometry into ONE token** → the seven `%23e6e6e6` glyph re-emits are now a **SECURITY REQUIREMENT**, not a tidy-up. *(Lesson worth keeping: **an access rule is only real if the data model can express the distinction it draws.**)* **Locked BEFORE implementation — `state.space_theme` is in NO Rust, NO TS, NO Svelte. `theme-*.css` DOES NOT EXIST. No milestone may claim theming works, and none may ship a Layer-3 applier that does not implement Ch6 §6.3.2 in full.** **Ch6 §6.2's four-layer model (D-057/D-058) is superseded IN PART** — intent survives (minimal reset, 13px/1.35, 4px unit); **`tokens.css` never existed, and component `<style>` blocks are FORBIDDEN, not required** — *the rule that makes skinning TOTAL is the rule that forbids the component from participating in it.* **Still open:** the exact colour-token allowlist · user-disable of Space themes.  

> **🔒 D-111 — A CLIENT MUST NOT FETCH A HOST CHOSEN BY SOMEONE ELSE (J-506, Ch2 v1.3, N-103).** **⚠️ OPENS WITH A RETRACTION — J-504/J-505 listed *"`url()` fetches"* as an open trust surface. IT IS NOT ONE.** Under **D-110's own** colour-only allowlist, `CSS.supports('color','url(…)')` is **false** — a `url()` **cannot enter a Space theme**. Fonts are **bundled** (Ch6 §6.2). *A threat named from pattern-matching, in the same doc that forecloses it — the SECOND overclaim in two turns on one topic. Retracted, not hedged.* **🔑 THE REAL INVARIANT, and the protocol had ALREADY WON IT:** *a client that fetches a URL chosen by **someone else** is a **BEACON** — it leaks the reader's **IP** and **read-time** to a host of the **sender's** choosing. **XGen publishes your XGID by design; it does NOT publish your network location.*** **Every avenue is already shut, structurally:** `message.image`/`message.file` carry **`xgen://hash/sha256:<64-hex>` — a CONTENT ADDRESS, NOT A LOCATION** (`xgen-core/src/blob_store.rs`; federation-native M12; per-blob client-encrypted; store *"content-blind by construction"*) · **no HTTP client in any crate** · fonts bundled · themes URL-free. **🔑 A HASH CANNOT NAME A HOST — the beacon is not *blocked*, it is UNSAYABLE.** *Content-addressing was taken for integrity and bought privacy for free; nobody had written it down.* **⚠️ THE ONE ROW THAT INVITES IT BACK: `link previews`** (Ch2's *"Client decisions — implementation freedom"* table). Unbuilt — but a client fetching preview URLs from message text hands the **sender** every reader's IP + read-time, invisibly, on every message. **✅ RULE: link previews — and ANY rendering that resolves an outbound URL — are fetched NODE-SIDE, NEVER client-side.** The Node already talks to the world; the Client deliberately does not. One fetch per link, not one per reader. *Freedom preserved: **whether** and **how** to preview stay the client's; only the fetch **LOCATION** is fixed — because it is not a rendering decision, it is **a privacy boundary wearing a rendering decision's clothes**.* **⚠️ WHAT ACTUALLY REMAINS, AND IT DWARFS EVERY GLYPH AND TOKEN: D-036 MODULE WIDGETS** — third-party HTML in an isolated webview; **CSP/sandboxing still OPEN at Ch6 §6.8.8** (filed Session 2, untouched since). **Unlike blobs, themes and glyphs, it has NO structural foreclosure at all.** Ch6's, not the UI track's. **Filed, not solved.** *Standing lesson: **a threat list padded with a foreclosed item is worse than a short one** — it trains the reader to skim, and the real entry gets skimmed with it. **Retract, don't hedge.***  

> **✅ D-112 + D-113 — THE PLUGIN TAXONOMY CLOSED AND THE MODULE-UI SANDBOX FLOOR LOCKED (J-507, Ch6 v0.6, `docs/xgen-plugin-taxonomy-phase0.md`).** **Locked together — you cannot classify a thing while leaving open what it is allowed to do.** **🔑 THE FRAME WAS ALREADY IN THE CODE:** `xgen-common/src/module.rs` (SE-D2) already says *"one unified handshake mechanism; the code term `kind` carries the system/ui distinction — a **module** is a *system* plugin (`host = node`), a **plugin** is a *ui* plugin (`host = client`)"*, plus **UUIDv4 slot/impl GUIDs that never federate** and *"the descriptor is a **const in the plugin's own code** — there is **no manifest file**; metadata is authoritative, location is never trusted."* *(Honest limit: the vocabulary is shipped; the `host`/`kind` FIELDS are not — `Descriptor` is `{kind_id, impl_id, name, assurance}`.)* **D-112 — ONE plugin, THREE axes:** **`host`** (node = system · client = ui) · **`delivery`** (`compiled` · `service` · `packaged`) · **`surface`** (`none`·`region`·`shelf`·`window`, at most one). ***"Module" and "widget" are not two species.*** **The drift was never Ch6-vs-D-102 — it was §6.8 vs EVERYTHING THAT WAS BUILT** (§6.8 predates the spine, the Auth registry, the widget tier, the region model and `WidgetMount`; **§6.8 moved**). **🔑 THE SLOT INVENTORY IS NOT A RIVAL — WE ALREADY BUILT IT:** half regions, half **content anchors inside a host widget**, and `message.details: WidgetMount[]` + unknown-id drop (W-13) **is** `room.message.decorator` **under another name** → **ONE placement model + ONE containment model. A slot is declared by the HOST, never requested by the guest.** **A THIRD species neither spec listed:** the **Auth Module is `delivery: service`** (`AuthModuleXgid` + `endpoint_url` + revocation — Ch6 §6.8.7 **corrected**). **Settings = `surface: window`; NO `screen` kind** (Discord full-overlay foreclosed knowingly — reversible on one word). **🔒 D-113 — THE BOUNDARY IS *DELIVERY*, NOT "WIDGET":** `self-panel` needs no CSP; **being `packaged` is what is dangerous** — the question sat on the wrong noun for three months. **S-1: a packaged module UI is a webview with NO NETWORK** (only its own local IPC channel) → **D-111's beacon is UNSAYABLE inside a module** · S-2 packaged assets · S-3 own origin, **no Tauri IPC** · **S-4 the module NEVER holds the key** (consent ≠ key handover; **our Rust signs, per event**) · **S-5 no trust chrome** (never the identity/verified/**AI-badge** zone — D-110 generalised) · S-6 deny-by-default caps · **S-7 no `packaged` plugin loads until S-1…S-6 ship** (**zero lines exist today → the lock costs nothing and forecloses everything**). **CLOSES Ch6 §6.8.8's widget-sandboxing question (open since April 2026)** + *module signing* + *module permissions*. **⚠️ NAMED, NOT SOLVED: the `compiled` LOADER is still open (`temperature.rs`) — a DYNAMIC LIBRARY IS NOT A SANDBOX; whichever loader wins must land on the delivery axis, and if it admits third-party code it inherits D-113.** **✅ surfaces §6: ①②③ CLOSED · ⑤ STRUCK (D-108 makes glyph provenance a BUILD FAILURE, not a design question — verified, not assumed) · ONLY ④ top-shelf pinning open, and it GATES NOTHING. → M-RP6.1i–l UNGATED.** **M-RP6.1i ✅ CLOSED (J-508): `shelf` + `shelf-face` core** — the **33rd + 34th** `core`; an ordered `role="toolbar"` command strip framing the widget grid (top favourites / bottom system) + its native-`<button>` leaf composing `icon`. **Roving = `menu-bar`'s LINEAR rove COPIED, NOT extracted** (4th independent impl — `entity-panel`/`menu-bar`/`menu`/`shelf`, **D-069's bar met**; extraction filed as **M-RP-ROVING**; `menu-bar`/`menu`/`entity-panel` untouched). **`aria-disabled` NOT native `disabled`** (the `menu-item` pattern → a disabled face stays **keyboard-reachable**, the state that makes 6.1j's all-disabled bottom shelf reachable; guarded `onclick` covers native Enter/Space). `onCommand` seam = the `menu-bar` seam (one command, two triggers). > **🔒 M-RP6.1k — DESIGN LOCKED 2026-07-12 (J-510 → D-114 + D-115). Runbook `tasks/M_RP6_1K_UISTATE_STORE.md` handed to Clair. 🟢 PLAY.** **🔑 TWO SPECS DISAGREED ABOUT WHO PERSISTS THE LAYOUT, AND GROUNDING — NOT RE-DERIVATION — SETTLED IT.** region-dock §9 specified a **layout-only** store (`xgen-client_layout.json` → `xgen-client_layouts.json`, five verbs, auto at **M-RP7.3** / named at **M-RP7.6**); surfaces §4 specified a **UI-state** store. **They were never two objects — §9 is simply the EARLIER, NARROWER DRAFT**, written before window geometry, the shelf, or a named-workspace concept existed. **🔒 D-114: ONE store — `xgen-client_uistate.json`.** §9's identity/reconcile rules **survive verbatim** as the `layout` key's own (`widgetId` durable · drop unknown · re-inject `system` · `version`+migrate); its **two filenames, its five verbs, and its “the layout manager is itself a widget” line are SUPERSEDED** (region-dock **v1.8**). Auto-save/load **and** named states are **both pulled forward from 7.3/7.6 into 6.1k** — S-8 already locked manual **named** states as *permanent, not a stopgap*, and the shelf faces dispatch them. **🔑 THE CARVE-OUT — AND IT IS THE REAL CONTENT OF THE LOCK: §4's inherited *“Rust persists an OPAQUE blob, never learns the shape”* rule CANNOT hold uniformly, and §4.2 is what breaks it.** The monitor-work-area **clamp is MANDATORY**, and **only Rust can read a work area, or apply a rect before the webview exists** (apply it later and the window visibly jumps). ***A clamp Rust cannot perform is not a clamp.*** → **`geometry` = a TYPED Rust struct** (`x,y,w,h,maximized`) **· every other key = an opaque `serde_json::Value`, round-tripped verbatim.** **This is J-499's rule APPLIED, not excepted:** J-499 killed `get_layout` because Rust would have **duplicated a type the webview owns** — **geometry is the OPPOSITE case**, a type **Rust already owns and the webview cannot**. ***Rust owns what only Rust can do, and stays blind to what the webview owns.*** *(A rule stated once and then applied without asking which side of it each thing falls on is how a rule becomes a superstition.)* Free consequence: an opaque value **preserves unknown keys** → every future key is additive with **zero Rust change**. **⚠️ AND THE FINDING THAT SHAPED THE MILESTONE — GREPPED, NOT ASSUMED: FIVE OF THE SIX §4.5 KEYS HAVE NO LIVE SOURCE TODAY.** Only **window geometry** has a feeder (resize grip + native decorations — the user moves it every session). **grid layout** = a **const** (`DEFAULT_LAYOUT`; no user mutation until renderer B) · **shelf favourites** = waiting on ④ pinning (the top strip mounts empty) · **collapsed/expanded** = **ZERO `collapsible` props anywhere in `ui/client`** (the 8 leaves are non-collapsible `section`s) · **theme** = `theme-*.css` **DOES NOT EXIST** (D-110) · **last open room** = no room selection until **M-RP6.2**. → **6.1k SHIPS `geometry` + `layout` ONLY, AND RESERVES NOTHING.** An empty `theme: null` key is the ***`tabs`-branch / N-091 shape at file scale*** — **a key nothing writes is a key nobody has round-tripped**; each remaining key lands **with the milestone that creates its source**. *(§4.5 is honoured, not narrowed: §4.5 locked what BELONGS in a UI state; **D-065 governs WHEN each one is built**.)* **🔒 D-115 — M-RP-WINSTATE's own written criterion FIRES → B** (own it; **`tauri-plugin-window-state` NOT taken**; M-RP-WINSTATE → **⬛ SUPERSEDED, absorbed**). **Unit = PHYSICAL px** (N-092b, measured twice) — ***a rect can only be compared to a monitor work area in the same unit, so a logical rect makes the mandatory clamp unimplementable***. **Clamp-don't-refuse EXERCISED** (write an off-screen rect, launch, watch it centre). **⚠️ AND N-095's DoD MOVES FROM M-RP7.3 TO 6.1k:** its deferral's premise was that `loadLayout()` **cannot return null** — and **6.1k is the milestone that kills that premise** (it is where `loadLayout()` stops returning a constant and starts parsing a real file). Leaving it at 7.3 would mean **7.3 closing a hole 6.1k opened**. ***A deferral is valid only as long as its premise holds; when the premise dies, the deferral dies with it — it does not quietly inherit a new one.*** **RENAMED before the commands ever existed (2 lines): `layout.save`/`layout.load` → `uistate.save`/`uistate.load`** — **the store is NOT a layout** (it holds geometry, and will hold shelf/theme/room); `layout.*` would be a lie by 6.2. **No `saveAs` id** — one diskette, one dialog, two outcomes. **LEGS — VISIBLE FIRST (Joe's standing brief: see the UI early, correct it while the milestone is open):** **A** save + load **dialogs** (shell-local, the `about-dialog` precedent) + **both commands enter `commandTable`** + **`diskette`/`load` FLIP ENABLED**, against an in-memory store → *the milestone's UI is on screen and correctable before one line of plumbing exists* · **B** Rust `get_ui_state`/`set_ui_state` + the file + the `loadLayout()` body swap + **N-095 EXERCISED** · **C** geometry save (`CloseRequested` + debounced move/resize) + restore + **clamp EXERCISED** · **D** named states carry geometry (§4.2) + session key + CDP verify → close. **Real client 9222 ONLY** — no sampler cells; **the sampler catalogue must stay 328, and that is a verify leg**. **`cargo test` MUST MOVE from 1507/0/62** — the honest signal that Rust landed (the inverse of the 6.1i/6.1j *“identical to baseline PROVES no-Rust”* leg). **Client registry baseline: 46, read QUIESCENT (N-105).**

Then Legs B/C/D shipped persistence and nobody swept it** — so the milestone reached close-out with a store that survives a relaunch **while the app told the user their workspace was not being saved.** Caught in the **painted DOM** at Rule-5 re-drive, with two named states sitting on disk to prove the contradiction rather than argue it. ***A STALE HONESTY NOTE IS STILL A FALSE STATEMENT — and it is WORSE than a missing one, because it was written by someone being careful, so the next reader trusts it.*** **The discipline already existed and was not generalised:** 6.1j bound *“the disabled face is a COUNTDOWN, not a resting state”* — **a W-8 disclosure is the same object.** The face got swept **because the DoD named it**; the note got missed **because nothing named it**. → **RULE: no milestone closes leaving its own UI asserting a phase-limit it has since removed. It was the cheapest defect in the milestone and **the only one that would have reached a human**.)* **🔒 D-114 realised LITERALLY:** `get_ui_state -> String` / `set_ui_state(json: String)` — a **raw-string opaque round-trip**; **ZERO descriptor types in the Rust crate** (the only `widgetId` in `xgen-client` is a raw string *inside a test*, which is the **proof of opacity, not a breach**); `WindowGeometry {x,y,width,height,maximized}` is the **only** typed part. ***Rust owns what only Rust can do, and stays blind to what the webview owns.*** **🔒 D-115 realised:** physical px throughout; clamp-don't-refuse **exercised** (off-screen rect → centred); **N-095 relocated here and exercised** (corrupt store → `DEFAULT_LAYOUT`, region-shell present, **no blank centre** — the J-499 30→21 failure **proven absent**); **`tauri-plugin-window-state` NOT taken**. **RESERVE NOTHING held — and it is visible on disk:** top-level keys are **exactly** `version`/`session`/`named`/`active`. No `theme`, no `shelf`, no `collapsed`, no `room`. **D-101 untouched** (config only) — the store is the project's **first deliberately persistent user-facing state**, by design. **🔑 N-108 — THE REGISTRY BREATHES WITH *STORE CONTENTS*, NOT JUST MENU STATE (extends N-105):** the Load dialog's picker/Load/Delete sit inside `{#if entries.length}` → quiescent count is **55 empty-store** and **59 with a saved state**. ***A baseline that depends on a DATA FILE is a baseline that can be wrong on a machine where nothing is wrong*** — **assert what is IN THE STORE, not merely that the popups are shut.** *(J-509's phantom one layer down.)* **Measured as a TRANSITION in ONE session — 55 → save → 59 → delete → 55**, exact return, zero leaks; a number **derived** by arithmetic was **refused entry to the record until it had been seen**. **🔑 N-107 — TWO WRITERS, ONE FILE:** Rust merges `session.geometry`, the frontend merges `named`/`active` — **read-modify-write on BOTH sides**, neither serialising a whole-file model it does not own. ***Any format with more than one writer must be MERGED, never REPLACED*** — whole-file serialisation by a narrow writer is **data loss wearing a save button's clothes**. > **✅ M-RP6.1l CLOSED (J-513) — THE PLUGIN LIST SHIPS. Commit `1dc5849` (6 files, +272/−17). 🟢 NEXT-ACTIVE = M-RP6.2 — R1 Spaces + R2 Rooms on real `KnownSpace`. BACK TO THE GRID.** **The gear is enabled; NO shelf face in the app is disabled** — the 6.1j countdown is discharged. **Shipped: the FIRST plugin registry in the project** (`ui/common/lib/plugins/registry.ts` — **D-112's three axes `host`·`delivery`·`surface` in code for the first time**), with **`widgetRegistry` DERIVING from it** (*a widget is in the grid **because** it is a registered plugin with `surface: region`*), and a **read-only pane** (5th widget, `kind: system`, `surface: none`) in a shell-local modal. **Three honest rows, `[system]` badge, no faked verbs.** The grounding held: **there was no registry to enumerate, so the milestone created the first one** — it did not project one out of Rust (J-499's rule; the inverse of D-114's geometry). **⚠️ CORRECTION 1 — A COUNTDOWN NAMES *WHO* FLIPS A FACE, NEVER *WHEN*. THIS MILESTONE WAS BUILT TOO EARLY AND THE RECORD IS WHY.** 6.1j wrote *“no face is enabled before its command exists, **and no milestone closes leaving its own face disabled** — 6.1k flips diskette/load, 6.1l flips gear.”* **Those are TWO rules wearing one sentence.** The first is a real guard (**never enable a control that resolves to nothing**). The second is a **SCHEDULE**, smuggled in beside it unexamined — and it **pulled a milestone forward because a face was waiting, not because the product needed it.** Joe: *“I honestly thought we would grey the widget manager till we have a working grid.”* **He was right; the schedule was Chat's.** → ***A disabled face with a NAMED OWNER is honest indefinitely. A countdown names WHO discharges it — never WHEN, because that turns a guard into a deadline and lets the shelf drive the roadmap.*** *(Kept, because it is not nothing: the derive is the seam every future region drops into, and it is verified. The sequencing that produced it is not something to be pleased about.)* **🔒 CORRECTION 2 — J-512's D6 IS SUPERSEDED (Joe). A GREYED BUTTON IS NOT ONE THING.** D6 refused Remove/Disable/Launch/Settings outright (*“no verb → ships ABSENT, not faked”*) — **too blunt: it collapsed two controls that look identical and are opposites.** **Grey because the verb was never built = a DEAD CONTROL** (it lies by implying the capability exists). **Grey because the plugin's OWN DESCRIPTOR says so = W-13, RENDERED** — *a disabled **Remove** on a `[system]` row **is the information**, and Ch6 §6.8.5 drew exactly that.* → **THE RULE: every button's state is DERIVED FROM THE DESCRIPTOR, never hardcoded, and a control is disabled only for a reason TRUE OF THAT PLUGIN and legible to the user** — `Remove` ⇸ `kind==='system'` (W-13) + confirm-on-click for `custom` (S-6) · `Disable` ⇸ `kind==='system'` · **`Launch` rendered ONLY IFF `surface==='window'`** (element-absent, not greyed) · `Settings` ⇸ `!hasSettings`. **⚠️ `Settings` is the honest-looking trap** — today it would be grey because **we never built a settings mechanism**, not because these plugins have none: **same grey, DIFFERENT TRUTH** → `hasSettings` rides the **descriptor**, and **the milestone that makes it true owes the button a target, written into its own DoD** (N-109). **→ M-RP6.1m (the action row) FILED + ⏸️ POSTPONED, NOT NEXT: four buttons, ZERO live feeders** (no settings mechanism · no `window` plugin · no disable verb · no `custom` plugin to remove) — ***precisely* the 6.1k finding** (*five of six §4.5 keys have no feeder*), and it takes the same answer: **each control lands with the milestone that creates its source.** **🔍 TWO RECORDS SURFACED BY SEARCH THAT NOBODY WAS READING. ① N-007** (`ui/docs/xgen-ui-notes.md`, 2026-06-02) filed *“every module needs a UI representation in both apps — including **system** modules”* (install/enable/disable/select · **status/health** · **a warnings home** — the vanilla EventStore's *“storage heavy”* contract *“needs a UI home so it isn't only a log line”*), marked ***“graduates into Ch6 + the module-framework milestone”* — AND IT NEVER GRADUATED.** Neither the taxonomy Phase-0 nor J-512 cited it; **the shipped list satisfies about a third of it.** *A note that files an obligation and never graduates is a note the project has decided to forget slowly.* **② THE SETTINGS-MECHANISM COLLISION — FILED, DELIBERATELY NOT DECIDED:** **(A)** Ch6 §6.8.2/§6.8.5's > **🔒 THE DOCK ENGINE (RENDERER B) — PHASE-0 LOCKED 2026-07-12. `docs/xgen-dock-engine-phase0.md`. THE CHAPTER THE WHOLE M-RP7 ARC RESTS ON — READ IT IN FULL BEFORE PROPOSING ANYTHING IN THIS ARC.** Renderer A (`region-shell`, M-RP6.1f) renders a **const** tree into flex boxes and **nothing mutates it** — *it looks like a dock and is a picture of one*. Renderer B turns it into one: tile frame, fold, splitter resize, drag-to-rearrange. **It is a RENDERER UPGRADE, not a region rewrite** — both renderers read one D-103 descriptor. **🔑 §0 — JOE'S AUTONOMY GRANT: Chat holds full authority over every mechanical aspect of this arc — descriptor, mutation algebra, gesture semantics, persistence, verification, milestone split — EXCEPT THE GRAPHICAL APPEARANCE OF THE CREATED ELEMENTS, WHICH IS JOE'S.** And per **N-090 appearance is WIDE**: gaps, spacing, sizing, tracks and layout are skin-tunable, so **stripe height, grip size, seam thickness, band thickness, the folded form and the hole raster are all Joe's, not Chat's.** *The grant is over mechanics, not over the record — findings, deviations and gaps are surfaced as always (D-065).*  

> **🔒 D-116 — THE DOCK REARRANGES; IT NEVER JOINS. LOCKED (Joe, 2026-07-13).** **A TARGET TILE IS AN ADDRESS, NOT A CONTAINER.** In a space-filling tree there is no empty space to drop *into* — the tree's only vocabulary is **relative**: *above that one · below · left · right*. **The drag verb is not "put A into B"; it is "insert A here, and *here* is expressed as an EDGE of B".** The target receives nothing; it is a **lookup key**. → **NO centre drop-zone** (the centre of a tile is **inert**) · **NO tabs, ever produced** · **NO docked/undocked mode** (a mode was only ever needed to discriminate split-vs-tab; and *a region is docked iff it appears in the layout tree* — **a query, not a flag**; two sources of truth for "is this docked" is a D-067 drift surface) · **NO `M-RP-ROVING` prerequisite** (a tab strip would have been roving's **5th** independent impl; it is not built, so the extraction stays filed and stays out of this arc). **Fold IS the stacking mechanism and it is the better one:** four folded stripes in a column is a tab strip lying on its side — except **several can be open at once** and **every label is always visible**. ***Tabs is what you reach for when you don't have fold. We have fold.*** **The door stays SHUT, not LOCKED:** `types.ts` keeps typing `tabs`, `resolve.ts` keeps dropping it with a DEV warn — zero cost, zero schema change if it is ever wanted. **⚠️ If a future milestone reaches for tabs it RE-OPENS §3 EXPLICITLY — it does not arrive as a rider on a drag milestone.** **⚠️ AND D-116 IS NOT WEAKENED BY HOLES (J-514/J-515):** §2's *"there is no empty space to drop into"* is now only *mostly* true — but **D-116's ground is Joe's own constraint (*"never mixing or joining"*), NOT the geometry.** The rhetoric was corrected; the decision stands untouched.  

> **✅ M-RP7.1 — THE TILE FRAME: STRIPE, GRIP, FOLD — CLOSED (J-514). Fifth in one family: N-091 · N-097 · N-099 · N-109 · this. **A verified claim is only ever as wide as the case that was actually run.**)* → **new milestone: M-RP7.1b.** **⚠️ D-117 WAS DRAFTED AND DELIBERATELY NOT LOCKED** — Joe superseded it before it reached `DECISIONS.md`, and his word on the replacement was ***"honestly i have to see it in practice."*** **→ no fold decision enters the record until it is built and looked at.** *A `D` locked for a design its author says he needs to see first is a prediction wearing a decision's clothes.* **MEASURED (Chat re-drove every leg, Rule 5):** client registry **67 — quiescent, EMPTY store, NO SELECTION** · **⚠️ N-108 EXTENDED — THE REGISTRY BREATHES WITH THE *SELECTION* STATE TOO: 67 (none) → 71 (selection) → 65 (selection + self folded). A baseline that does not state its SELECTION state is unreadable** → every future baseline states **store state AND selection state** · `cargo test` **1517/0/62 IDENTICAL** (*proves* no Rust) · `npm test` **43** · `vite build` **168** · sampler catalogue **328** · ratios `[1,2,7,2]` exact at **1242px and 1252px**. **⚠️ FILED, NOT FIXED — A LIBRARY-WIDE DEFECT: `mergeClasses` DOES NOT DEDUPE**, and `envelope` already stamps the type-class from `name` (N-023) → **any component that ALSO writes a literal `class="X"` renders `class="X X"`.** Five registered elements do it today: `region-shell` · `self-panel` · `inspector-panel` · `combobox` · `plugin-list`. **Clair fixed `region-tile` only — correctly, that was her scope.** The sweep (or making `mergeClasses` dedupe — a **`$common` base change**) is **ITS OWN MILESTONE, NEVER A RIDER.** **TOOLING, measured not folklore:** ⚠️ **the CDP bridge WRAPS getters** — `get(id)` returns `{type, state}`; read **`get(id).state.foo`** (reading `get(id).foo` returns **all nulls, which looks exactly like a broken build**) · tile classes are `region-tile-stripe`/`-move`/`-title`/`-fold`/`-body`/`-resize` (**not** `-strip`/`-grip`) · ⚠️ **an attribute selector with an unquoted `#` throws a bare `EVAL ERROR`** (quote it, or iterate `querySelectorAll('[data-debug-id]')`) · escaped double-quotes inside `-Expression` break the harness (use single quotes in the JS, or `String.fromCharCode(39)`) · `npm test` lives in **`ui/sampler`**, `vite build` in **`ui/client`** · ⚠️ **`cargo test` EXCEEDS THE MCP TIMEOUT — run it DETACHED and poll the PID in SEPARATE SHORT CALLS**; a long `Start-Sleep` kills the shell **and takes the detached run with it**, leaving a truncated log that read **1195/0/60 — plausible, complete-looking and WRONG**, betrayed only by the **missing final `test result:` line**. ***A killed detached run leaves a MEASUREMENT-SHAPED ARTIFACT.***  

> **✅ M-RP7.1b — THE FOLD AXIS BECOMES THE USER'S CHOICE; SPLITS SHRINK-WRAP; THE HOLE GETS A FLOOR. CLOSED (J-516).** Design locked J-515; shipped `0f25e50` + `14eb4d8` [Clair] + the appearance fix [Chat]. Phase-0 → **v1.4**. **⚠️ STILL NO FOLD `D` — Joe has now SEEN it run and has not asked for the lock. D-116 stands alone.** Runbook `tasks/M_RP7_1B_FOLD_AXIS.md` (COMPLETED). **Zero Rust · zero sampler · zero node**, verified by diffstat. **🔑 THE FINDING WAS A SCREENSHOT.** M-RP7.1's own arc table said its purpose was *"this is where Joe sees it and corrects the appearance"* — **he did, and it corrected more than the appearance.** He folded two regions, saw the empty band, and raised three things; **they turned out to be one thing.** **⚠️ The holes are NOT caused by the new design — they are IN THE SHIPPED BUILD** (N-111). **① THE FOLD AXIS IS THE USER'S CHOICE — TWO BUTTONS:** `[<]` folds left (collapses **width** → vertical strip) · `[v]` folds top (collapses **height** → horizontal stripe) · **folded ⇒ the unused button is DISABLED, the used one unfolds.** **Two axes, NOT four directions.** *(Cost, honestly: §4.1's elegant free property — "drag a folded tile col→row and it re-orients itself" — **DIES**. **The right trade: it was elegant for the TREE and surprising for the PERSON.** And it finishes a sentence §4.1 only half-wrote — it made fold **available** everywhere and left the **direction** an accident of the tree.)* **`collapsed` stops being a flag and becomes a DIRECTION (`'width' | 'height'`) — STORED, because it is user intent, and user intent is not derivable from anything.** The **along/across mode** stays **derived** (a fact about the tree). **② 🔒 A SPLIT SHRINK-WRAPS WHEN ALL ITS CHILDREN FOLD ACROSS IT (§4.4) — the part Joe did not ask for, and the part that actually solves his screenshot.** **Fold is a LEAF verb; a split's width is a SPLIT property living in the PARENT's `sizes[]` — a leaf verb cannot reach a split property**, so the 2/12 column stayed 2/12 wide no matter how its children folded. **❌ `collapsed` on a SPLIT node was REJECTED on Joe's own lock:** a leaf takes its title from `CLIENT_PLUGINS.name` and **a split has NO NAME** → name field + naming UI + **chrome on every split, nested, forever** = ***a group container promoted back to a MAIN FORM***, the exact thing §5 killed. **✅ Instead:** all children folded **across** ⇒ the split shrink-wraps, **the weight returns to its siblings by the `flex` they already have**, unfold one child ⇒ it re-inflates, **weights never mutated**. **Costs NOTHING NEW:** no descriptor field (**derived** — a stored flag goes stale the instant a child unfolds, **D-067 in miniature, twice in one arc**), no split chrome, and the mechanic is **byte-for-byte the folded leaf's** (`flex: 0 0 auto`). **⚠️ Mixed folds (`[<]`+`[v]`) deliberately DO NOT shrink-wrap** — column stays wide, hole opens, raster explains it. ***No magic, no guessing what they meant.*** **③ 🔒 HOLES ARE LEGAL, PAINTED, INERT (§4.5).** **§7.1's *"no holes, rectangles only"* is AMENDED by the man who accepted it 24 h earlier, in the open.** The hole is **flex leftover space inside a split, not an element** → **the raster is a BACKGROUND on the split container: zero new DOM, one skin rule**, shipped **provisional** (*you cannot tune a raster under holes you have not seen*). **🔒 AND THE LOCK FOR M-RP7.4: A HOLE IS NOT A DROP TARGET — D-116 says a target tile is an ADDRESS, and a hole has NO ADDRESS.** *(Drop on the EDGE of the tile above it. **Allow drops into holes and we have quietly built free 2-D placement and retired the tree.**)* **⚠️ D-116 IS NOT WEAKENED — its ground is Joe's constraint, not the geometry. Correct the rhetoric; do not touch the decision.** **⚠️ NO `D` LOCKED, ON JOE'S WORD: *"honestly i have to see it in practice."*** D-117 is dead without ever having been written, **and the replacement is NOT being locked either.** Phase-0 v1.3 carries the design — *that is what a Phase-0 is for*; `DECISIONS.md` carries **what Joe has decided, and he has told us he has not.** ***A `D` locked for a design its author says he needs to see first is a prediction wearing a decision's clothes.*** **🔑 TWO GROUNDED FINDS THAT CHANGED THE RUNBOOK BEFORE IT WAS WRITTEN:** **① `migrate` DOES NOT EXIST** — grepped: the word appears **only in comments** (`types.ts:16-17`, `layout-default.ts:63`); **`version` has been bumped TWICE and there has never been a migrate FUNCTION.** → **7.1b CREATES it.** *A path described in three documents and implemented in none is not a path; it is a plan.* **② ⚠️ A LAYOUT CAN ALREADY BE ON DISK, and the Phase-0 said the opposite BY ACCIDENT** — §12's *"nothing writes `session.layout`"* is **true**, and it was **read as "nothing writes a layout"**, which is **false**: **`app_client.svelte:227` has persisted layouts inside NAMED UI STATES since M-RP6.1k.** Measured on disk: `named: {}` → **migration is free TODAY — BY LUCK, NOT BY DESIGN; one diskette click ends that.** → **the answer is not "don't click the diskette", it is WRITE THE REAL MIGRATE** (`v2→v3`: each `collapsed: true` leaf takes its parent's `dir` → explicit direction — the old derived rule made honest, ~10 lines) **— DoD: EXERCISED in vitest against hand-built `v2` trees under BOTH parent kinds. Fed, not asserted (N-091, applied to the one branch in this codebase that has NEVER ONCE RUN).** **🔒 §4.1 AND §4.4 SHIP TOGETHER, NEVER APART — two buttons without the shrink-wrap is a thin strip beside a huge hole, STRICTLY WORSE than today.** ***Shipping the disease and the cure a week apart is how a bad appearance gets defended.***

READ THIS BEFORE THE NEXT LEG.** **① THE MIGRATE RAN — FOR THE FIRST TIME EVER.** Grounded before the runbook: **`migrate` DID NOT EXIST** — the word was **only in comments**; `version` had been bumped **twice** with **no function at all**. *A path described in three documents and implemented in none is not a path; it is a plan.* **`migrateLayout` is now real**, six vitest cases fed with hand-built `v2` trees — **and Chat DROVE IT LIVE** through the **real Load dialog** (plant a `v2` named state → shelf face → combobox → Load → `handleUistateLoad`). **Clair's hand-back said the live leg was not drivable; it was — the way in was three clicks, not a new harness.** *A branch you cannot reach through the product is a branch you have not tested.* **Two properties only the live run showed:** a migrated `v2` layout is **HOLE-FREE BY CONSTRUCTION** (the old derived rule only ever folded ALONG → **nobody's saved workspace can come back with a hole in it**), and **the migrate NEVER REWRITES THE STORED STATE** — a **read-path transform**; the record stays `v2` on disk and re-migrates every load. **Idempotent, non-destructive, never silently edits a file the user wrote.** **② ⚠️ THE DEVIATION WAS CORRECT AND THE RUNBOOK WAS WRONG.** Clair flagged (Rule 6) `migrateLayout(raw, **fallback**)` instead of the runbook's bare `migrateLayout(raw)` → **`resolve.ts` is `core` and `DEFAULT_LAYOUT` is SHELL-LOCAL; B would have pointed `>`, at the content). **Only the HEIGHT button spoke disclosure** (`v` = *"my body is below"*) — straight from Joe's original `[<][V]` sketch, **which mixes the two conventions in one stripe** — **and the skin comment directly above the rotations already described convention A correctly.** ***The label was wrong, the prose was right, and one rotation value contradicted both.*** **🔒 THE RULE: THE CHEVRON ALWAYS POINTS WHERE THE REGION WILL GO IF YOU PRESS IT** — enabled or disabled, folded or not; the **disabled** button keeps its open glyph, still truthfully naming what it WOULD do. **The fix was TWO ROTATION VALUES SWAPPED.** → **N-116: re-reading the chapter would NEVER have caught this — the CHAPTER was self-consistent and the CODE was not.** **④ `--region-fold-gap: 0` is a KNOB, not a reservation — and ONLY because of `display: inline-flex`.** On the shipped `display: inline` a `gap` is **INERT**, and **an inert knob IS a reservation** (§4.3.1 — RESERVE NOTHING). **The test: can Joe change the value TODAY and see the render move? Yes.** **⑤ 🔑 A FOURTH REGISTRY AXIS, AND IT LIVES ON DISK (N-115): ONE SAVED UI STATE = +4** (`textfield#uistate-load-pick__input` · `combobox#uistate-load-pick` · `button#uistate-load-go` · `button#uistate-load-del` — **element-absent when there is nothing to pick**, which is CORRECT per J-500). **→ `67` IS ONLY TRUE WITH ZERO SAVED UI STATES. One diskette click and the client reads 71 FOREVER** — N-108's shape, except **the "data file" is one the USER writes with a button.** **⚠️ AND `71` IS NOW AMBIGUOUS: selection-active = 71; zero-selection + one-saved-state = 71. SAME NUMBER, TWO CAUSES.** **Axes: quiescence (N-105) · store (N-108) · selection (N-112) · named-state count (N-115).** **⚠️ One anomaly FILED, NOT EXPLAINED (Joe: *"leave it right now"*):** one launch rested at **71** with Joe's **real identity** auto-selected; three later fresh launches all rested at **67, no selection**. `self-panel` selects on `onActivate` (a click), never on mount — **something activated it and Chat could not establish what. > **⏸️ `M-RP-SKIN` — THE APPEARANCE PASS. FILED (Joe, 2026-07-13): *"majority of graphical elements will be changed or updated after ui mechanics completion."*** **🔑 THIS IS THE NAMED DISCHARGER FOR EVERY `PROVISIONAL` MARKER IN THE GRID ARC** — the fold chevrons, stripe/grip/triangle sizing, the folded strip's form, **`--region-pad` / `--region-seam`**. **⚠️ ONE EXCEPTION (2026-07-14): NOT the hole raster — its discharger is `M-RP-PLATE`, which REPLACES it rather than tuning it.** **The countdown rule satisfied at ARC scale instead of per-element: WHO = Joe, WHICH MILESTONE = `M-RP-SKIN`.** *It is not a deferral of a decision — **you cannot tune an appearance whose mechanics are still moving underneath it.** Every provisional in this arc now points somewhere; none points at nothing.* **⚠️ SKIN ONLY** (`skin.css` + tokens, N-090/N-025). **If it turns out to need a component change, that is a FINDING, not a licence — the component change is its own milestone.**  

> **⏸️ `M-RP-PLATE` — THE GRID BACKDROP: AN INERT, LIVE-SWITCHABLE PLATE WIDGET UNDER THE TILES. FILED (Joe, 2026-07-14). Phase-0 §4.5.1.** Joe: *"the space under regions aka 'the hole' will be customizable — from solid black to animated reactive colour fractal clouds. What is in the hole right now is a **dev plate with no background defined**. The real one we do by a **background widget** which sets it by its own setting."* **🔑 THE MECHANISM ALREADY SHIPS, ONE LEVEL DOWN — grounded, not recalled:** `message-stream.svelte` carries **`background?: WidgetMount[]`** — an **ARRAY** (so a *stack* of plates is free) · `position:absolute; inset:0` · **`pointer-events: none`** · W-13 unknown-id drop · a **`backgroundLive`** switch passed into every mount (*"a reactive widget renders frozen when false; a static object ignores it"*). Locked J-481, shipped J-482 — **chat wallpaper. This is grid wallpaper: THE SAME SEAM.** *"Solid black" is a `div` with a colour; "fractal clouds" owns a canvas; **the host never learns the difference.*** **🔑 IT COSTS NO SURFACE, AND THE TAXONOMY ALREADY SAID SO:** W-12 = at most one of `region·shelf·window·none`, and a backdrop declares **none of them** — not a gap but **surfaces §3.2: content inside a HOST is not a surface** (the `temperature-indicator` ruling). **No tile, no region, no shelf face, no new surface kind.** **🔑 A HOLE CANNOT HOLD ANYTHING** — no element, no address, no identity (*which is exactly why D-116 holds and §7.1 survived*) → **the plate is a BACKDROP, NOT A HOLE-FILLER: ONE plate, `inset:0`, behind the whole `.region-shell`** (not one per split — a cloud that restarts at every split boundary reads as N surfaces, not one). **The tiles are OPAQUE, so what shows through IS the holes** — and the `--region-pad` perimeter with them. **🔒 IT MAY READ THE POINTER; IT MAY NEVER CAPTURE IT** (`pointer-events:none`, passive listener only) — ***the instant a hole is clickable it has an ADDRESS, D-116 falls, and §7.1's lattice argument is live again. A reactive backdrop is fine; a clickable one retires the tree.*** **⚠️ IT RETIRES §4.5's *"zero new DOM, one skin rule"*:** a CSS background does solid/gradient/pattern/keyframes, but **NOT a canvas, a shader, or anything reactive** — the dynamic plate **is an ELEMENT**. → **today's dot raster is the placeholder the plate REPLACES, not the seed it grows from** — but it is **PROMOTED, NOT DELETED**: it becomes the first **system** plate widget, so the socket ships **FED** (an unfed branch is an unverified branch, D-065/N-091). **⚠️ THE GATE IS A LOCK, NOT A PREFERENCE:** *"…sets it by its own **setting**"* — **there is no settings mechanism, deliberately.** J-513 filed the Ch6 `settings_schema`-vs-plugin-component collision as **explicitly undecided**, binding: ***nothing is built toward either until the grid works.*** **Joe: *"that is why i don't want to solve it now. now has priority widget grid with functional empty regions. background widget we can create after the grid concept works."*** **🔒 THIS ARC RESERVES NOTHING FOR IT** — no prop on `region-shell`, no descriptor key, no store, no manifest slot (*a key nothing writes is a key nobody has round-tripped* — the M-RP6.1k finding). **ZERO IMPACT ON M-RP7.2.**  

> **🎛️ THE GRID'S TWO SPACING KNOBS — NEW (Joe, 2026-07-14). Phase-0 §4.5.2.** Grounded: `.region-shell` had **NO padding** (and `.app-center` sets `padding: 0` explicitly, J-499's D5), and `.region-split` carried a **hardcoded `gap: 1px` — not even a knob**. Both are now **PROVISIONAL** tokens on `.region-shell`: **`--region-pad: 6px`** = the gap between the **GRID and the FRAME EDGE**, set **non-zero on purpose so it is visible for development inspection** · **`--region-seam: 1px`** = the gap **BETWEEN tiles**, same hairline, same value, now tunable. **🔑 `--region-seam` SURVIVES M-RP7.2:** the flex `gap` becomes a real seam **ELEMENT** (a `gap` cannot be hit-tested) and **this token becomes its thickness** — one token, both eras, so retuning it never means finding two places. **🔒 THE PERIMETER IS NOT A SEAM AND CAN NEVER BECOME ONE:** seams exist **only BETWEEN a split's children** (the `{#each}` interior) — **there is no leading or trailing seam** — so the outer edge can never grow a drag cursor or a hit zone. *It costs the splitter milestone nothing and it cannot lie to the user about a drag that does not exist.* **Ratios UNAFFECTED** — flex distributes over the reduced content box, so `[1,2,7,2]` still holds **exactly**; only the absolute px shrink by `2×pad`. Global `box-sizing: border-box` (`xgen-normalize.css:21`, grounded) means the padding cannot overflow the frame. **Registry unchanged. No component, no Rust.**  

> ### 🔑 **N-120 — THE ONE DEFECT M-RP7.2 SHIPPED. CLAIR FILED IT "LATENT, OUT OF SCOPE"; CHAT REACHED IT; IT INVERTS A RESIZE. → A REQUIRED LEG OF M-RP7.3, NOT A FILED ITEM.**
> **`region-node` threads `path` from the RESOLVED child index; `resizeSplit` walks the DESCRIPTOR by that path** — and **`resolve.ts` DROPS** (unknown leaves · `tabs` · all-dropped splits). **Any drop and the two index spaces diverge.** **REACHED, NOT ARGUED:** plant ONE unknown `widgetId` → descriptor has 3 children, **2 tiles paint, 1 seam** (between `spaces` and `rooms`) → **drag it RIGHT to ENLARGE `spaces`** → descriptor `[1,1,1]` → **`[1660,340,1000]`**: **`spaces` HALVED (660→335px) and `rooms` GREW.** ***The resize did the exact opposite of the gesture*** — the pair resized was `ghost ↔ spaces`. **And 55% of the row's weight now belongs to a widget that does not exist** — which **M-RP7.5 would write to DISK**, and which would **reappear eating half the screen** if that widget returned. **🔒 THE ASYMMETRY, PROVEN IN THE SAME POISONED LAYOUT: FOLD IS DROP-SAFE (it addresses by `regionId` — an IDENTITY); RESIZE IS DROP-FRAGILE (it addresses by `path` — a POSITION).** ***A position is only an address in the tree it was counted in.*** **🔑 THE RULE, BIGGER THAN THIS ARC: a DERIVED VIEW MAY RENUMBER — an index into a derived view is NOT an address into the source.** Either the resolved node carries its **source index**, or every mutation addresses by **identity**. *`resolve.ts` already knows the descriptor index at the moment it drops — it just throws it away.* **⚠️ Unreachable in today's build** (all 8 ids registered, `tabs` never produced — D-116) — **reachable the first time a widget id is RETIRED OR RENAMED and a user loads a saved workspace** (the W-13 reconcile case; M-RP7.1b proved the Load dialog is three clicks away). ***"Unreachable today" is the argument that has now been wrong FIVE times here*** (N-091 · N-097 · N-099 · N-109 · N-116). **→ M-RP7.3 OPENS WITH IT: addressing IS algebra, and a misaddressed `resize` nudges two integers while a misaddressed `move` RELOCATES A PANEL INTO THE WRONG BRANCH.**  

> **🧪 `cdp-debug.ps1` GAINS `-KeepSelection` (J-519) — because A HARNESS THAT CAN ONLY PASS IS A WEAK HARNESS.** The harness was clearing the selection before every gesture, so it **AVOIDED** N-118 instead of **TESTING** it. **CONTROL (clear suppressed): two drags on the tile body → `mousedown, mousemove, dragstart` — AND THEN NOTHING. No `mouseup`.** **N-118 reproduced ON THE WIRE, with the native drag finally VISIBLE** (at J-518 it was only *inferred*). **SUBJECT: the seam, same conditions, a live 21-char selection → both consecutive drags COMMITTED, no `dragstart`, `mousedown: false`** — because **`preventDefault()` on `pointerdown` SUPPRESSES the compat mouse events** and the pointer events carry the drag. **Measured, not assumed.**  

> **⚠️ THE M-RP7.1 SE CORNER TRIANGLE IS STILL PAINTED-DEAD, AND ITS DISCHARGER SAID "M-RP7.2".** This milestone resized on the **SEAM**, not the **CORNER**. **The countdown is RE-POINTED, not quietly dropped** → **`M-RP-SKIN` decides whether a corner grip exists at all; if it stays, it needs a milestone that makes it LIVE.** *A countdown whose milestone came and went without discharging it is exactly the painted-dead chrome this project keeps refusing (J-500 · 6.1j).*  

> **🎛️ THE GRID'S SPACING — ONE TOKEN, `--region-gap: 5px` (Joe, 2026-07-14; J-520). Phase-0 §4.5.2.** **⚠️ J-517 shipped TWO tokens at TWO values** (`--region-seam: 1px`, `--region-pad: 6px`) — **Joe found it by looking at the screen** and drew two arrows: *the regions were glued to each other and floating off the frame.* ***A gap that means one thing at the edge and another between tiles is not a knob; it is a bug with two names.*** **Both now DERIVE from `--region-gap`** and must never drift apart again. **RE-DRIVEN: edge 5 · seam 5 · tile-to-tile 5 · grab zone 8px · registry 67 · MID-drag descriptor still `[1,2,7,2]`, AFTER `[198,102,700,200]` · behaviour-neutral.** **`--region-seam-hit` 3→1px** — it was sized for a 1px seam; against a 5px one it would reach into each neighbour's edge and eat clicks (§0, mechanical).  

> ### 🔑 **THE BACKDROP MOVED: `.region-split` → `.region-shell` (J-521). Joe: *"the not-transparent padding around whole grid is still there."***
> **He was right, and the width was never the problem — the SURFACE was. MEASURED:** the **split** painted `--s5` + the raster; **`.region-shell` AND `.app-center` were BOTH transparent.** → ***the gap BETWEEN regions showed the GRID's backdrop, and the gap AROUND the grid showed THE ROOM BEHIND THE GRID.*** **Two different surfaces for one conceptual gap.** *Joe's phrase — "the edge gap is made from outside" — described the implementation exactly, from the picture alone.* **🔒 NOW: the SHELL paints · the SPLITS are TRANSPARENT · the TILES are OPAQUE ⇒ every gap (seam · hole · perimeter) is the SAME ONE SURFACE.** **🔒 AND THIS IS ALREADY THE `M-RP-PLATE` ARCHITECTURE, arrived at early and for free** — that milestone now only swaps a `background-image` for a real element. **⚠️ `.region-split` MUST NEVER GET A BACKGROUND AGAIN** (it would re-split the surface *and* block the gap model). **⚠️ NEAR-MISS: a two-hunk `Filesystem:edit_file` failed on its SECOND hunk, so NEITHER applied — while a separate edit removing the old background DID.** For one build **nothing painted the grid at all.** ***edit_file is all-or-nothing PER CALL, not per hunk — and the CDP read caught it, not the diff. A diff that looks applied is not a render.***  

> ### ⚠️ **AND THE PATTERN IS THREE-FOR-THREE IN THIS ARC: N-118's two wrong diagnoses (written into the harness as "measured"), and now a wrong DEPENDENCY (written into three canonical records with a 🔒 on it). ALL THREE WERE INTERNALLY CONSISTENT. NONE WAS CHECKED AGAINST THE THING IT CLAIMED ABOUT.** ***A dependency you have not TRIED TO BREAK is a dependency you have ASSUMED.*** *N-116: the record can be self-consistent while the code is not. N-118: my reasoning can be. This: my ARCHITECTURE can be — and a lock icon does not make it true.*  

> **⏸️ `M-RP-RESTART` — FILE ▸ RESTART **AND** REVERT. FILED (Joe, 2026-07-14; J-520). LANDS WITH M-RP7.5.** Joe asked for a File ▸ Restart item, *"disabled in states where it cannot be executed — no problem for me."* **The guard is right; grounding it split the item in TWO.** **🔑 `Restart`** (bounce the process) is **real today** — **`tauri-plugin-process` is ALREADY in `xgen-client/Cargo.toml`** and that plugin *is* `restart()`/`exit()`. **⚠️ BUT IT IS DECLARED AND NEVER WIRED** — no init in any `xgen-client/src/*.rs`, no frontend use: **a dependency nothing consumes.** **⚠️ `Revert`** (*"no automatic save on exit, load last automatic save"*) **— THERE IS NO AUTOMATIC SAVE: the grid does not write `session.layout` until M-RP7.5.** → **"restart without saving" and "restart" are the SAME THING today, and `Revert` would be greyed 100% of the time — not "in some moments".** ***A permanently-disabled item is not a guard; it is a promise*** — the painted-dead chrome this project keeps refusing (J-500 · 6.1j), and **the identical reason M-RP7.6's grid lock cannot ship early.** **→ ship both LIVE, together, with M-RP7.5** — the milestone that creates the state they discard. **⚠️ Named now: after M-RP6.3 (the send verb) a restart tears down a live WS that may hold UNSENT MESSAGES.** *Restart gets more expensive the more the app actually does.*  

> **🎨 THE REGION'S OWN EDGE — `--region-border: 1px` · `--region-edge: var(--s4)` (Joe, 2026-07-14; J-522). Joe: *"give each region 1px very fine smooth border. When gap is 0, there is no sense of regions, all is melted."* He tested the extremes (0 and 20px) and settled on `--region-gap: 4px`.** **⚠️ THE COLOUR COULD NOT BE `--s5` ANY MORE — J-521 MADE `--s5` THE BACKDROP**, so an `--s5` edge is **invisible at any gap > 0**. The edge must read against **both** neighbours: darker than the backdrop (`--s5 #343b47`), lighter than the tile body (`--s #16181c`). **`--s4 #2a2f38` does both.** *A token that was correct for a hairline stopped being correct the moment the surface behind it changed.* **🔑 N-121 — AND A REAL `border` BROKE THE ARC'S BASELINE. MEASURED FIRST: `[1,2,7,2]` came back `[1, 1.97, 6.90, 1.97]`.** With `flex: n 1 0` flex distributes the **FREE** space by weight, and then each item's **BORDER BOX = content + 2×border** — a **constant** added to every tile. **Content boxes stay proportional; border boxes do not — and EVERY rect JS can measure is a border box.** **⚠️ WORSE THAN COSMETIC: the splitter computes its drag fraction from the neighbours' `getBoundingClientRect()` widths, so a border would have biased the resize arithmetic by `2×border` per tile — systematically, silently, forever, and nothing would ever fail.** **✅ FIX: `outline` + `outline-offset: -1px`** — identical hairline, **ZERO layout impact**; **re-measured `[1,2,7,2]` EXACT**, `--region-min` still 22, folded strip still 22px with **zero overflow**, clamp still stops at 22 without folding. *(An inset `box-shadow` is also layout-free but paints BELOW the children — the stripe's `--s2` would cover the top hairline. An outline paints ABOVE. ⚠️ Tiles are not focusable today; if one ever needs a focus ring it must NOT be an outline on this element.)*  

> **🔑 N-122 — AND JOE'S OWN EXTREME TEST HAD A HOLE HE COULD NOT SEE.** He set `--region-gap: 0`, saw it render, and reported *"the system withstands it"*. **It renders — but the SEAM ELEMENT *IS* `--region-gap` WIDE, so at 0 the seam is ZERO PX**, and `--region-seam-hit` was a **constant 1px** → a **2px grab target: alive, and ungrabbable by a human.** *He tested that it did not break; he did not test that he could still drag it.* **✅ THE HIT AREA NOW COMPENSATES FOR THE GAP:** `max(1px, calc((8px - var(--region-gap)) / 2))` → **a CONSTANT grab zone at every setting. MEASURED (`elementFromPoint` swept at each value): 9px @ gap 4 · 9px @ gap 0 · 22px @ gap 20 — and THE DRAG COMMITS AT GAP 0** (MID `[1,2,7,2]` untouched, AFTER `[180,120,700,200]`). ***A knob that silently kills a gesture at one end of its range is not a knob.*** **⚠️ `calc()` IS SAFE HERE ONLY BECAUSE THIS TOKEN IS NEVER READ BY JS** — `getComputedStyle` does **not** resolve `calc()` in a custom property; it returns the raw token stream and `parseFloat` gives **`NaN`**. **`--region-min` and `--region-snap` ARE read by JS and must stay PLAIN VALUES.**  

> **⚠️ N-123 — AND THE INSTRUMENT THEN BROKE JOE'S TOKEN, AND HE REPORTED IT AS A BUG IN HIS OWN CSS.** Joe: *"something happens, `--region-gap: 4px` doesn't work anymore."* **It was not his CSS.** Proving the drag at gap 0 (N-122) needed the token set in **one** CDP call and the drag in the **next**, so the override could not be undone inside a single eval — it went on as an **inline style** on `.region-shell` **and was never removed.** **⚠️ VITE'S HMR HOT-PATCHES CSS WITHOUT A RELOAD, SO THE INLINE OVERRIDE SURVIVED EVERY EDIT HE MADE** — inline beats the stylesheet; his 4px was applied and then silently overridden, indefinitely, **by a leftover from a test he never ran.** **🔒 RULES (in `CDP_DEBUG_HARNESS.md`): a probe that persists a mutation across calls OWES A CLEANUP CALL, and the cleanup is PART OF THE PROBE · any session that touched inline styles ends with `location.reload()` · prefer reloading between token variants over inline overrides.** ***N-118 said the instrument can lie to ME. N-123 says it can lie to JOE — and he has no way to tell that the thing on his screen is not the thing in the file.***  

> ### 🔑 **AND THE THING IT WAS LOCKED TO FIX DOES NOT EXIST (N-124). ⚠️ "tile↔hole is 0" IS NOT OBSERVABLE.**
> The claim sat in the skin, in Phase-0 §4.5.2, in this PLAY block, and in the milestone's own runbook — **which Chat wrote before measuring it.** **A/B (inject the OLD geometry live, fold a tile ACROSS to build a real hole): folded-tile insets 4/4 vs 4/4 · distance-to-hole 774.8 vs 774.8 · the full gap census 4 EVERYWHERE under BOTH.** **🔒 Since J-521 moved the backdrop to `.region-shell`, A HOLE AND A GAP ARE THE SAME SURFACE** — a tile "butting onto a hole" is indistinguishable from a tile with a gap, *because there is nothing on the far side of that gap to be separated from.* **ZERO VISUAL DELTA.** **FOURTH TIME, SAME SHAPE:** N-118 my *reasoning* · J-521 my *architecture* · N-116 the *record* · **N-124 the *justification*** — and a 🔒 icon changed nothing except how confidently it got repeated.
>
> **🔒 JOE SHIPPED IT ANYWAY, ON THE RIGHT REASON: IT DELETES A MECHANISM, NOT A TOKEN.** The perimeter was the *shell's padding*, the inter-tile gap the *seam's thickness* — **two mechanisms, two tokens, which had already drifted apart once** (J-517: 1px vs 6px; Joe caught it by looking). **They can never drift again, because they no longer exist.** And **a gap that lives on the REGION travels with it when M-RP7.4 moves it**; a gap that lives on the GRID exists only where the grid puts a seam.

> ### ⚠️ **N-124a — TWO BASELINE NUMBERS IN THIS ARC WERE MORE PRECISE THAN THEIR MEASUREMENT. USE THE NEW ONES.**
> **`[1,2,7,2]` WAS NEVER BIT-EXACT.** Recorded "EXACT" at four window widths; at full float precision it is **`[1, 2.000112, 7.000224, 2.000112]` — and the OLD geometry returns `2.000114`.** ~0.004px of **pre-existing Chromium sub-pixel, in BOTH models** — **which is exactly what proves the margin adds no bias** (N-121's border broke it by **1.5%**; this is **0.006%**). **And the clamp stops at `22.29px`, not "exactly 22"** (integer-weight rounding, L2; the OLD geometry rounds to **21.89 — just BELOW the floor**). ***A number you rounded before you recorded it is a number you can no longer use as a control.***
>
> **MEASURED (Chat re-drove every leg, Rule 5):** perimeter **4/4/4/4** · **14 adjacent tile pairs all exactly 4**, incl. pairs crossing a split boundary (**it composes at depth**) · **MID-drag** descriptor `[1,2,7,2]` untouched with the button still down, **AFTER** `[194,106,700,200]` (pair total 300 invariant, untouched siblings ×100) · grab zone **9px @ gap 0 · 9px @ gap 4 · 20px @ gap 20**, seam wins at x=113–115 *inside* the neighbour's border box (**N-119's `z-index` re-proven**) · registry **67** (the zero-width seam registers nothing) · `npm test` **59** · `vite build` **169** · zero Rust **by scope**.

> ### 🟡 **THE DESIGN WALK BEHIND IT — SIX MEASURED FINDINGS AND TWO JOE CORRECTIONS (J-598).** ① **THE OUTAGE IS BIDIRECTIONAL** — a send is queued as **intent, not an event** (`resident.rs:705-727`; the drain builds/signs/anchors at write time), so during an outage **the user's own message never becomes an event at all**; no persistent outbox; `SendOutcome` stays honest. ② **THE CATCH-UP MECHANISM IS BUILT, TESTED AND NEVER CALLED BY THE GUI** — `SyncRequest`→`HistoryBatch`+`SyncComplete` (`app.rs:1752-1784`), `collect_sync_history` (`fanout.rs:478`) member-only/paginated/cursor-resumable, five passing tests, used by `ai_service.rs` + `batch.rs`; **`resident.rs` never issues one**, and no sync cursor is persisted. ③ 🔑 **THE CLIENT HAS NO MESSAGE HISTORY AT ALL** — `ingest` is in-memory, capped `INGEST_CAP = 500`, with a `dropped` counter: **a session window, not a cache**; the startup block does **no history fetch of any kind** ⇒ **every start is a cold start with an empty conversation**, and the blackout gap is a special case of a permanent one. ④ 🔒 **SYSTEM ENTRIES ARE THREAD ENTRIES LOCAL TO ONE USER (Joe, WhatsApp *delete-just-for-user*)** ⇒ **the rendered thread is a PER-USER PROJECTION of the shared record, not the shared record**; four consequences agreed — the thread becomes a **merge**, ordering meets two clocks, persistence is session-scoped **but COUPLED to message persistence** (or a reopened thread shows a gap with its explanation missing), and **additive and subtractive are ONE facility** so delete-for-me lands in the same local layer. **Drafted `D-130`; number and wording Joe's.** ⑤ 🔒 **NO MESSAGE CACHE (Joe): *"the client is just reader-sender. doesnt hold any users data."*** 🔑 **The lock sits a LEVEL ABOVE the question, so the D-093 crypto-shred tension never has to be adjudicated — the copy that would create it never exists.** ⚠️ **One measured exception: the address book DOES persist third-party data**, bounded by `T1_DEFAULT_RETENTION_DAYS = 182`; ⚠️ **whether eviction is WIRED or merely DECLARED is UNMEASURED — Chat's.** ⑥ 🔒 **THE OUTBOX SURVIVES THAT LOCK** — Joe's *"purge … not whole enblock"* maps onto the four-way `SendOutcome`: **purge on `accepted`, keep on everything else**; per-item is load-bearing because a block purge discards a `timed_out` beside a confirmed one. 🔑 **An outbox item BECOMES a cache the instant the node accepts it — which is exactly when the rule drops it.** 🔓 Open: post-reconnect anchor timestamp · `timed_out` auto-retry vs manual · the sibling's name. → J-598.

> ### 🔑 **H1 / H2 — THE ADDRESS BOOK AT REST, AND THE ONE PLACE THE THESIS ACTUALLY BITES (J-598).** ⚠️ **THERE IS NO IDENTITY LOOKUP VERB ON THE WIRE — NONE.** Records are **derived from a Space DAG drain** (`fill_and_members`, `desktop.rs:644-697`), independently confirming `M_RP_ADDRESS_BOOK.md` §5. ⇒ Joe's *send pubkeys, get visit cards* is **new protocol** (wire `xgen-core/src/wire/types.rs:45` · node handler · **and the SPEC CHAPTER — in a protocol project the spec IS the deliverable**). **H1 — drop the persistence, keep the book in memory — needs NO protocol at all** and is nearly free: `loadMembers` fires the fill **unconditionally on every Space change**, so the file only buys the pre-first-fill window and restarts. 🔒 **Joe relaxed the resource lens** (*"i dont mind if we will need to add some cli … we will test it"*) — build cost is **not** the objection. 🔑 **THE THESIS ANSWER, ASKED IN THOSE TERMS: THE VERB IS THESIS-NEUTRAL; THE SCOPE RULE IS THESIS-BEARING.** No-anonymity means *within a Space you know who you are talking to*, **NOT** *any identity is globally queryable by anyone holding a pubkey* — **an unscoped verb converts the first into the second: a thesis change made by accident, at the wire level.** Member-scoped, as `collect_sync_history` already is. ⚠️ **Recorded as Chat's READING from the project record — the Ch0–Ch2 read is OWED before any of it reaches a spec.** 🔓 H1 and H2 are Joe's and belong to neither current milestone. → J-598.

> ### 🟢 **§5b BELAYED — THE ROW SHAPE IS DROPPED BECAUSE THE STATE IT DRAWS CANNOT CURRENTLY BE REACHED (J-649, 2026-08-01). Phase-0 v1.5 → v1.6 · M13 v1.2 → v1.3 (§3c NEW) · parent v1.13 → v1.14 · ROADMAP v6.36 → v6.37. NO CODE; NO FLOOR MOVED.** 🛑 **JOE DREW THE E2 ROW — struck name · id beneath · own CSS class — THEN ASKED WHETHER THE CASE WAS ALREADY DEFINED ELSEWHERE. CHECKING THAT PRODUCED TWO ANSWERS, AND THE SECOND STOPPED THE WORK.** ✅ **ANSWER 1 — THE VOCABULARY EXISTS AND IS NOT OURS TO TAKE:** `.entity-avatar[data-revoked]` (`skin.css:2400-2417`) already ships **greyscale + 0.55 opacity + a diagonal slash**, exactly the language drawn. 🛑 **But `revoked` SHIPS UNFED (N-097), belongs to M13, and `D-127` separates the states — a revoked identity returns its record WITH `revoked` set; `not_found` is reserved for ERASURE.** ⇒ **squatting it would make the two indistinguishable the day M13 lands.** 🛑 **ANSWER 2 — ③ IS UNREACHABLE FOR ANYONE YOU HAVE INTERACTED WITH. Two measured facts compose: a held identity is NEVER re-fetched** (`partition_observed`, `ops.rs:2764`; the doc at `:2752` says so outright) **and a held record is NEVER removed in production** (`remove` `:253` and `evict_older_than` `:285` are built and correct — **every caller is a test**; §6's E1/E2/E3 erasure semantics are **wired to nothing**). ⇒ **once the book holds someone it holds them forever and never asks again** ⇒ **`identity.not_found` can only fire for an identity whose record was NEVER cached.** 🔑 **FOR A DM YOU HAVE ACTUALLY USED, THE FIRST FILL CACHED THE COUNTERPART — THEIR ERASURE IS PERMANENTLY INVISIBLE AND E2's ROW NEVER APPEARS.** 📌 **Reachable in exactly ONE situation: a client with no cached record — fresh install, new device, wiped book.** Real, and it is the **multi-device** case — but it means ③ is an artefact of *never having known someone*, **not of learning that someone went away.** ✅ **§5a's E2 LOCK STANDS AND NEEDS NO REWORK** — it is the right rule *when* the state occurs; **only the visual design is belayed.** ✅ **AND THE BELAY COST NOTHING — IT SAVED:** the two-line row, the id line and the L1/L2/L3 variant question are dropped, so **`M_RP_MEMBERS` §7/L10's *secondary·meta·status UNFED* lock STAYS CLOSED** and **§6a's `tail-8` gap stays FILED** instead of becoming a precondition. 🔑 **A QUESTION THAT COST ONE TURN SAVED FOUR OPEN ITEMS.** ⚠️ **CHAT'S SKETCH WAS WRONG TWICE AND A LOCK WAS NEARLY TAKEN FROM IT:** ① **it drew the `card` layout in a panel that renders `variant="row"`** — `showSecondary` is card-only (`entity-item:69`) and `.ei-meta` is `flex:none; margin-left:auto` (`skin.css:2469`), so **under `row` the second field is pinned to the RIGHT EDGE of the same line, never below the name** ② **it struck through a display name that CANNOT EXIST** — with no book record `toDescriptor` already falls back to `tail(m.identity_id)`, so **an erased row has no name to strike; the xgid tail IS its name.** 🔑 **THE SKETCH WAS HAND-WRITTEN HTML, NOT THE COMPONENT.** It was the right tool for *"should the row show at all"* — which prose had failed to settle — **but it is NOT evidence about what the shipped component can render.** ⚠️ **A SKETCH MUST BE CHECKED AGAINST THE COMPONENT BEFORE ANYTHING IS LOCKED FROM IT.** ⚠️ **AND AN INVERTED PARAPHRASE IN TWO OF CHAT'S DOCUMENTS:** both said the `tail-8` clip *"clips the **left**"* — **the INVERSE of the truth.** `.ei-name` is `overflow:hidden; text-overflow:ellipsis; white-space:nowrap` — **LEFT-ANCHORED, clipping the RIGHT**, which is why the constant `ed25519:` head survives and the distinguishing bytes die. ✅ **The canonical source §6a was ALWAYS correct** (*"the clip then takes the WRONG END"*); > ### 🟢 **§5a THE DM EXCEPTION — HIDING IS A SUBTRACTION UNTIL THE HIDDEN MEMBER IS THE WHOLE COUNTERPARTY (J-648, 2026-08-01). 🔒 E2 + P1 LOCKED · Phase-0 v1.4 → v1.5 · ROADMAP v6.35 → v6.36. NO CODE; NO FLOOR MOVED.** 🔒 **E2: ③ IS NEVER HIDDEN WHEN IT IS THE DM COUNTERPART.** §5's hiding stands everywhere else. 🔑 **THE RULE KEYS ON `is_dm`, NOT ON MEMBER COUNT — and that is what makes it a rule rather than a heuristic about size.** Measured: **`is_dm` is PROVENANCE**, written `true` once at Space creation (`state.rs:451`/`:567`) and **never recomputed from current membership** ⇒ **a GROUP room shrunk to two is NOT a DM and §5 hides there as locked**; a DM stays a DM after its counterpart is erased. 🔑 **WHY §5's OWN REASONING DID NOT REACH IT:** §5 was locked on *"there is no usage of such members"* — **an argument about GROUP ROOMS. In a DM the counterpart is not a member of the conversation; they are the other half of it.** The stream still renders their messages (G9), so hiding makes the panel claim **you are alone in a room whose other half is on screen one panel over**; and under §1 G3 an erased counterpart **can never reply**, which is the single most useful fact about the room. ⇒ 🔑 **THE RULE THAT GENERALISES: hiding is legitimate when it is a SUBTRACTION. When the hidden member is the entire counterparty, hiding is not a subtraction — IT IS A FALSE CLAIM THAT NOBODY WAS THERE.** ⚠️ **CHAT MIS-PRICED THE OPTIONS AND CORRECTED IT BEFORE THE RULING, so the lock was not taken on a false cost (`D-131`).** Chat had said E2 *"needs a word, which re-opens D-126's wordlist"* — **wrong; a MARK suffices.** ✅ **E3 is the option that cannot avoid the wordlist, and structurally so: the `NOTE` table (`members-panel.svelte:62-68`) is JOE'S COPY and every entry names an effect on US** — *"I am waiting" · "I cannot reach" · "I cannot see"* — **and for an erased identity NOTHING IS WRONG ON OUR SIDE, so no honest "I…" sentence exists.** E3 also needs a sixth panel state or a `message` that stops being a pure function of `panelState`. ⇒ **E2 is the more honest AND the cheaper of the two non-trivial options.** 🔑 **THE SKETCH IS WHAT CAUGHT THE MIS-PRICING — it survived two rounds of PROSE and died the moment a ROW WAS DRAWN.** ⚠️ **And the first sketch was itself defective: it drew E1 and E2 and OMITTED E3, one message after describing all three. Joe caught it — *"e3 is nowhere"*.** The second drew all three, with **E3's note rendered as `"?"` — the empty quote IS the finding.** 🔒 **P1 LOCKED — THE HOOK TRAVELS AS A PROP**, threaded `members-panel` → `entity-panel` → `entity-item`; `entity-item`'s root carries `data-variant`/`data-kind`/`data-selected` and **no unresolved prop**, so **TWO `core` components gain public API** (also consumed by `self-panel` + the sampler). **Rejected `EntityDescriptor`** — cheaper, but it describes **the entity** while this is a fact about **our knowledge of it**, the category error §2's tier frame exists to prevent and the same shape as the `display_name` claim retracted at J-644. **Rejected `flags`** — it feeds `entity-avatar` and the treatment belongs to the **ROW**; that route is exactly how `isAi` ended up collapsed at J-643. ✅ **THREE LEG-B CONSTRAINTS GROUNDED BEFORE ANY RUNBOOK. > **🔧 THE TRUSTED-MOUSE HARNESS (leg 0, J-518) — THE INSTRUMENT THE REST OF THE ARC RUNS ON.** `cdp-debug.ps1` has **`-Mode click`** and **`-Mode drag`** (real `Input.dispatchMouseEvent` ⇒ **trusted**; an `eval`-synthesised event is `isTrusted:false` and fires no native defaults — J-496) plus **`-MidExpression`, evaluated WHILE THE BUTTON IS STILL DOWN** — **not a convenience: "preview live, write the descriptor once on release" is UNPROVABLE if you can only read after `mouseReleased`.** **🔒 Coordinates are CSS pixels — the `getBoundingClientRect()` space. DPR 1.25 does NOT apply** (calibrated by clicking a fold button at its measured rect centre and watching `collapsed` flip). **⚠️ `button` must be `"none"` on a button-up move** or a HOVER reports `buttons=1` — harmless to a splitter, **would poison M-RP7.4's drop-band hover**. **⚠️ Integer coords only** — a PS `[double]` emits `123,5` on this sk-SK box, which is not JSON. **⚠️ Re-measure coordinates before EVERY gesture: a rect is not a constant** (folding moves the buttons; Chat dragged a stale coordinate during the J-519 re-drive and selected text instead). **⚠️ N-118 lives here: `-KeepSelection` exists so the harness can REPRODUCE the native-drag trap, not merely avoid it.**  

> **🔒 M-RP7.2 — THE EIGHT DESIGN LOCKS (taken under §0: mechanics are Chat's, only graphical appearance is Joe's).** **L1 — `mutate.ts` is BORN at 7.2** with `resizeSplit` only; **7.3 adds `move` + pulls `fold` out of the shell.** *⚠️ The arc table put the algebra AFTER the first mutation and that cannot be true: **the first mutation IS algebra.*** **L2 — INTEGERS ONLY** (§7 bans floats): resolution comes from an **exact integer scale-up** — `[1,2,7,2]` → ×100 → `[100,200,700,200]`, then **only the dragged pair moves**; untouched siblings keep their proportions **to the byte** and the pair's total is invariant, so **nothing else moves**. *Cost, stated: a saved workspace reads the scaled numbers after a drag — the price of §7's own lock.* **L3 — LIVE PREVIEW; integers written ONCE on `pointerup`** (a float never reaches the descriptor even if the user thrashes; the proof is ONE clean diff, not sixty). **L4 — a seam is draggable iff BOTH neighbours carry a main-axis weight** — one derived predicate; folded-along and shrink-wrapped fall out of it with **no special case**. **L5 — `path: number[]`, DERIVED. NO schema change** (a split id would be a key nothing round-trips — M-RP6.1k); **M-RP7.4's `move` needs the identical addressing, so it is paid once.** **L6 — the min-clamp READS THE SKIN** (N-090); **it stops, it never auto-folds** (§4.2). **L7 — NO keyboard resize** (a tab stop on all **7** seams nobody asked for, *and* it would dodge the harness this milestone exists to land). **L8 — THE SEAM DOES NOT REGISTER** (`.region-split` has no getter either — the seam is its chrome): **registry stays 67**; proof is the tree diff + painted geometry. **⚠️ TWO RETRACTIONS, BOTH MINE:** Chat **over-asked** — three questions to Joe, **two of them Chat's own to answer** under §0 (*autonomy is not a licence to be vague; it is an obligation to decide*) — and Chat **named a scrollbar-collision risk WITHOUT MEASURING IT** (rule ③ cuts both ways: **do not name a risk you have not grounded**).  

> **🎯 M-RP-FOCUS — FOCUS IS A STATE, NOT A BRAND (filed 2026-07-12, Joe-locked; PENDING, skin-only, gates nothing).** **The library focuses two ways today and one is wrong.** ✅ The **field family** (`textfield`/`textarea`/`number`/`date`/`select`/`select-multiple`/`color`/`converter-field`) already indicates focus with a **lighter-grey BORDER-LIFT** (`border-color: var(--t3)`, `outline:none`) — correct: quiet, legible. ❌ The **action/nav family — ~13 components** (`button`·`link`·`toggle`·`range`·`file`·`star-rating`·`file-field`·`section-toggle`·`cp-num`·`entity-panel-option`·`entity-context-menu`·`menu-trigger`·`menu-item`) still rides `--focus-ring`, a glow **hardcoded to `--accent2`**. **🔑 THE ROOT CAUSE IS A MISSING NAME:** there is **no `--focus` token** — the ring hardcodes the accent, and the correct field fix borrows **`--t3`, a TEXT-RAMP token** (so it currently means *"dim text"*, not *"focused"*). **A token's NAME is what stops the next person re-pointing it at the accent.** **Fix:** add **`--focus`** as its own semantic token (light grey; **NOT an alias of `--t3`/`--t4`, NOT the accent**) → point `--focus-ring` at it → **one line flips all ~13 off the accent with ZERO component edits**; field family takes `border-color: var(--focus)`. **Open at kickoff:** glow vs border-lift for the action family (*one treatment library-wide is cleaner — they have borders too*). **Supersedes “Q5 focus policy: accent” (J-412).** **Cost: `skin.css` ONLY** — no component code, no registry change (*the N-025/N-090 payoff made literal*). **⚠️ KNOCK-ON:** milestones asserting *“the focus ring is the ONLY accent carrier”* (menu-bar·status-bar·separator·M-RP6.1i) become *“nothing moves under an accent swap”* — **update the assertion; it is NOT a regression.**  

> **⚫ Track A (self-set status protocol) — CLOSED through PROTO-STATUS.2 (J-461, latest JOURNAL).** A parallel protocol arc run alongside the RP track: self-set status (emoji + description line) was found absent from the protocol → specced + built. `state.status/<identity_xgid>` object `{emoji?,text?,updated_at,expires_at?}` (global/public, presence excluded); new `xgen-core/src/status/` module (`StatusRecord` + validating ctor + `is_expired`, reuse `state.*` resolution + owner-write guard, 19 tests, 1502/0 workspace, Appendix I §V.4). Docs: `xgen-status-gap-phase0.md`, `xgen-proto-status-phase0.md`, `xgen-proto-status-spec.md`, `tasks/RUNBOOK_PROTO_STATUS_2.md` (COMPLETED). **Gates the status-bearing `entity-avatar` variants (M-RP5.2).** Deferred: per-space status, visibility gating, presence.
